(defprotocol mtls13 diffie-hellman
  (defrole client
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)
      (request response text) (x rndx) (y expt))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat sr (exp (gen) y)
          (enc (cat s spk (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash "sf"
              (hash "fin"
                (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) "s" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) "s" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc "cert" c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash "cf"
            (hash "fin"
              (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) "c" cr sr))
            (cat
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash "sf"
              (hash "fin"
                (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) "s" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c cpk
            (enc "cert" c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) "c" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))))))))
    (uniq-orig cr)
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)
      (request response text) (y rndx) (x expt))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat sr (exp (gen) y)
          (enc (cat s spk (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash "sf"
              (hash "fin"
                (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) "s" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) "s" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc "cert" c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash "cf"
            (hash "fin"
              (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) "c" cr sr))
            (cat
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash "sf"
              (hash "fin"
                (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) "s" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c cpk
            (enc "cert" c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) "c" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x)))))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))))))))
    (uniq-orig sr)
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false)))))

  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x)
  (goals
    (forall

          (ugen x) (non (invk spk)))

          (and (p "server" z-0 5) (p "server" "cr" z-0 cr)
            (p "server" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "server" "spk" z-0 spk)

            (p "server" "c" z-0 c) (p "server" "ca" z-0 ca)
            (p "server" "x" z-0 x) (p "server" "y" z-0 y))))))

            (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) "s" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc "cert" c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash "cf"
            (hash "fin"
              (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) "c" cr sr))
            (cat
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk

                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk

              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash "sf"
              (hash "fin"
                (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) "s" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c cpk
            (enc "cert" c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) "c" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))))))))
    (uniq-orig cr)
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)

    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))

    (uniq-gen y)

    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))

        (enc response
          (hash "s ap" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))))))))
    ((recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat sr (exp (gen) y)
          (enc (cat s spk (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash "sf"
              (hash "fin"
                (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) "s" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) "s" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc "cert" c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash "cf"
            (hash "fin"
              (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) "c" cr sr))
            (cat
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash "sf"
              (hash "fin"
                (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) "s" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c cpk
            (enc "cert" c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) "c" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request-0
          (hash "c ap" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x)))))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))))))))
    ((recv
       (hash "c ap" cr sr
         (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))))))
      (send
        (hash "c ap" cr sr
          (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x)))))))
    ((recv
       (cat "c ap" cr sr (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))))))
      (send
        (cat "c ap" cr sr
          (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x)))))))
    ((recv (cat "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x)))))
      (send (cat "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))))))
    ((recv (cat "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))))
      (send (cat "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x)))))
    ((recv (cat (exp (gen) x) y)) (send (cat (exp (gen) x) y))))
  (label 55)
  (parent 53)
  (unrealized (6 0))
  (dead)
  (maps
    ((0)
      ((spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (cr cr) (sr sr)
        (request request) (response response) (x x) (y y))))
  (comment "empty cohort"))

(defprotocol mtls13 diffie-hellman
  (defrole client
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)
      (request response text) (x rndx) (y expt))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat sr (exp (gen) y)
          (enc (cat s spk (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash "sf"
              (hash "fin"
                (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) "s" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) "s" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc "cert" c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash "cf"
            (hash "fin"
              (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) "c" cr sr))
            (cat
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash "sf"
              (hash "fin"
                (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) "s" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c cpk
            (enc "cert" c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) "c" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))))))))
    (uniq-orig cr)
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)
      (request response text) (y rndx) (x expt))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat sr (exp (gen) y)
          (enc (cat s spk (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash "sf"
              (hash "fin"
                (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) "s" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) "s" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc "cert" c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash "cf"
            (hash "fin"
              (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) "c" cr sr))
            (cat
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash "sf"
              (hash "fin"
                (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) "s" cr sr))
              (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash "scv" cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y) s spk
                  (enc "cert" s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))) c cpk
            (enc "cert" c cpk (privk ca)))
          (hash (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))) "c" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x)))))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap" cr sr
            (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul y x))))))))
    (uniq-orig sr)
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false)))))

(defskeleton mtls13
  (vars (cr sr request response text) (spk cpk akey) (s c ca name)
    (y rndx) (x expt))

    (x x))

  (uniq-orig sr)
  (uniq-gen y)
  (absent (y x))
  (traces
    ((recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat sr (exp (gen) y)
